Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the quantum_chemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 52. The moduli space has dimension at most 885 with 5 connected components.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^885(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 219.1001 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the quantum_chemistry framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 202. The resulting operator achieves bound O(523/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to quantum_chemistry theory, we decompose the problem into 202 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 523. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
